title: Durable original-pack model activation, cancellation and recovery
tool: native Mac checks and bounded process monitor
---

The Mac owner now persists requested, verified, loading, checking, committed and failed activation states under an exclusive device-local lease. Original-pack loading verifies files, plans against current headroom, completes a bounded text health request and flushes the receipt before publication. A failed replacement releases its partial allocation and can sequentially restore the previously healthy configuration, while failed-selection requests remain blocked. Explicit settings retry, cancellation and new-owner recovery are tested separately.

The first full Mac suite passed. A subsequent review identified two pre-publication gaps: opening the journal or writing initial intent could fail before setting the queue gate, and cancellation during durable commit needed another check before publication. The final source fixes both. The second suite preserved a compiler error in the new async assertion; the corrected third full Mac suite passes. The final frozen executable also passes a separately bounded real-model campaign, including cancellation in the committed-write callback, preservation of the previous receipt, complete release and a later successful retry. Earlier real activation and legacy memory-lifecycle results are retained against their own earlier binary.

These are functional original-pack lifecycle and resource checks, not speed, quality, alternative-pack support or public release qualification. No installed binary, weight pack, default quantization or user Home was replaced. The final tests used a disposable activation history and fixed small memory ceilings. No model ran concurrently with a build. Global paging remains diagnostic.

The static wrappers inherited an unused engine binary hash. Their commands invoke the Mac suite, not that engine executable. The final frozen build identity binds the actual Mac check executable, library, input manifest and source archive. App inputs match before and after each successful full suite; check/CLI sources were captured afterward while unchanged and are not claimed to have before/after coverage. The final light failure screen was visually inspected in addition to the scripted Light, Dark and System checks.

Local home prefixes are replaced with <HOME>. Original lengths and SHA-256 identify the unmodified files. Large normalized UTF-8 transcripts are losslessly zlib-compressed and base64 encoded. Decode with `zlib.decompress(base64.b64decode(block))` and check the normalized digest. Every encoded block is verified before writing. Frozen binaries, source archives and weights remain in bounded local staging; the receipts bind their exact bytes.

### .build/quantization-research/run-activation-real-v1.py

Original bytes: 3538. SHA-256: `41f8d7b776517578bf1a5e5569bc3e6c4be2f8875a69427f61f19e15e76c092a`.

Normalized bytes: 3538. SHA-256: `41f8d7b776517578bf1a5e5569bc3e6c4be2f8875a69427f61f19e15e76c092a`.

````text
from pathlib import Path
import ctypes,ctypes.util,hashlib,json,os,subprocess,sys,time
sys.path.insert(0,'Tools')
from context_qualification import quiet_preflight
from prefill_bench import terminate_child_tree,vm_snapshot
r=Path('.build/quantization-research').absolute();out=r/'activation-real-v1';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
cli=r/'frozen-activation-v3/sevra-mac-checks';binary=sha(cli)
assert json.loads((cli.parent/'build-identity.json').read_text())['binary_sha256']==binary
record={'schema':1,'complete':False,'qualification':False,'scope':'Functional actual-model activation and existing app lifecycle. Two sequential model processes, no timing qualification.','binary_sha256':binary,'metallib_sha256':sha(cli.parent/'mlx.metallib'),'build_identity_sha256':sha(cli.parent/'build-identity.json'),'resource_protocol_sha256':sha(r/'activation-resource-protocol-v1.json'),'runs':[]}
save=lambda:(out/'receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')
env=dict(os.environ)
for key in list(env):
 if key.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG','VQ_','VQLAB_')):del env[key]
env['SEVRA_DBMD']=str(Path.home()/'.dbmd/bin/dbmd')
commands=[
 ('unloaded',[str(r/'activation-no-metal-v3/checks'),'--performance'],6),
 ('activation',[str(cli),'--activation-real','--home',str(out/'activation-history')],10),
 ('legacy',[str(cli),'--performance-real','--home',str(out/'legacy-home')],10),
]
record['commands']=commands;save()
lib=ctypes.CDLL(ctypes.util.find_library('proc'));child=None;start=time.monotonic()
try:
 for name,command,limit in commands:
  before=quiet_preflight(limit+3);row={'case':name,'command':command,'before':before,'maximum_process_bytes':int(limit*1e9),'peak_tree_bytes':0,'samples':0};record['runs'].append(row);save();started=time.monotonic()
  with (out/(name+'.log')).open('w') as log:
   child=subprocess.Popen(command,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,start_new_session=True)
   while child.poll() is None:
    rows=[tuple(map(int,line.split())) for line in subprocess.check_output(['ps','-axo','pid=,ppid='],text=True,timeout=5).splitlines()];pids={child.pid}
    while True:
     more=pids|{pid for pid,parent in rows if parent in pids}
     if more==pids:break
     pids=more
    total=0
    for pid in pids:
     b=ctypes.create_string_buffer(296)
     if lib.proc_pid_rusage(pid,4,b)==0:total+=max(int.from_bytes(b.raw[72:80],'little'),int.from_bytes(b.raw[240:248],'little'))
    row['peak_tree_bytes']=max(row['peak_tree_bytes'],total);row['samples']+=1
    if total>limit*1e9:raise RuntimeError('process envelope exceeded')
    if vm_snapshot()['reclaimable_bytes']<3e9:raise RuntimeError('lost three-GB real headroom')
    if subprocess.check_output(['sysctl','-n','kern.memorystatus_vm_pressure_level'],text=True,timeout=5).strip()!='1':raise RuntimeError('OS memory pressure')
    if time.monotonic()-started>1800 or time.monotonic()-start>5400:raise RuntimeError('time envelope exceeded')
    time.sleep(.25)
   row['exit_code']=child.returncode;row['seconds']=time.monotonic()-started;row['after']=vm_snapshot();save()
   if child.returncode:raise RuntimeError(name+' failed; dependent phases not run')
   child=None

 assert sha(cli)==binary
 record['complete']=True
except BaseException as error:
 record['failure']=type(error).__name__+': '+str(error)
 if child is not None and child.poll() is None:terminate_child_tree(child)
 save();raise
finally:save()
print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in record.items() if k != 'runs'}))
````

````text
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0,'Tools')
from context_qualification import quiet_preflight
from prefill_bench import terminate_child_tree,vm_snapshot
r=Path('.build/quantization-research').absolute();out=r/'activation-real-v2';out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
cli=r/'frozen-activation-v4/sevra-mac-checks';binary=sha(cli)
assert json.loads((cli.parent/'build-identity.json').read_text())['binary_sha256']==binary
record={'schema':1,'complete':False,'qualification':False,'scope':'Functional actual-model activation and existing app lifecycle. One model process, no timing qualification.','binary_sha256':binary,'metallib_sha256':sha(cli.parent/'mlx.metallib'),'build_identity_sha256':sha(cli.parent/'build-identity.json'),'resource_protocol_sha256':sha(r/'activation-resource-protocol-v2.json'),'runs':[]}
save=lambda:(out/'receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')

for key in list(env):
 if key.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG','VQ_','VQLAB_')):del env[key]
env['SEVRA_DBMD']=str(Path.home()/'.dbmd/bin/dbmd')
commands=[
 ('activation',[str(cli),'--activation-real','--home',str(out/'activation-history')],10),
]
record['commands']=commands;save()
lib=ctypes.CDLL(ctypes.util.find_library('proc'));child=None;start=time.monotonic()
try:
 for name,command,limit in commands:
  before=quiet_preflight(limit+3);row={'case':name,'command':command,'before':before,'maximum_process_bytes':int(limit*1e9),'peak_tree_bytes':0,'samples':0};record['runs'].append(row);save();started=time.monotonic()
  with (out/(name+'.log')).open('w') as log:
   child=subprocess.Popen(command,env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,start_new_session=True)
   while child.poll() is None:
    rows=[tuple(map(int,line.split())) for line in subprocess.check_output(['ps','-axo','pid=,ppid='],text=True,timeout=5).splitlines()];pids={child.pid}
    while True:
     more=pids|{pid for pid,parent in rows if parent in pids}
     if more==pids:break
     pids=more
    total=0
    for pid in pids:
     b=ctypes.create_string_buffer(296)
     if lib.proc_pid_rusage(pid,4,b)==0:total+=max(int.from_bytes(b.raw[72:80],'little'),int.from_bytes(b.raw[240:248],'little'))
    row['peak_tree_bytes']=max(row['peak_tree_bytes'],total);row['samples']+=1
    if total>limit*1e9:raise RuntimeError('process envelope exceeded')
    if vm_snapshot()['reclaimable_bytes']<3e9:raise RuntimeError('lost three-GB real headroom')
    if subprocess.check_output(['sysctl','-n','kern.memorystatus_vm_pressure_level'],text=True,timeout=5).strip()!='1':raise RuntimeError('OS memory pressure')
    if time.monotonic()-started>1800 or time.monotonic()-start>1800:raise RuntimeError('time envelope exceeded')
    time.sleep(.25)
   row['exit_code']=child.returncode;row['seconds']=time.monotonic()-started;row['after']=vm_snapshot();save()
   if child.returncode:raise RuntimeError(name+' failed; dependent phases not run')
   child=None

 assert sha(cli)==binary
 record['complete']=True
except BaseException as error:
 record['failure']=type(error).__name__+': '+str(error)
 if child is not None and child.poll() is None:terminate_child_tree(child)
 save();raise
finally:save()
print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in record.items() if k != 'runs'}))
````

### .build/quantization-research/freeze-activation-v4.py

Original bytes: 2214. SHA-256: `6567919142238ad1e35130af7269a0b191cfccde07a47bb3223336e2601b614d`.

Normalized bytes: 2214. SHA-256: `6567919142238ad1e35130af7269a0b191cfccde07a47bb3223336e2601b614d`.

````text
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json,shutil,subprocess,tarfile
r=Path('.build/quantization-research');run=r/'activation-mac-final-v3';out=r/'frozen-activation-v4'
assert json.loads((run/'receipt.json').read_text())['complete']
before=(run/'inputs-before.json').read_bytes();after=(run/'inputs-after.json').read_bytes();assert before==after
app=json.loads(before);sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
for name,digest in app['files'].items(): assert sha(name)==digest,name
paths={Path(p) for p in app['files'] if not p.endswith('.metallib')}
for base in ['apps/macos/Checks','apps/macos/NativeChecks','apps/macos/CLI']:
 paths.update(p for p in Path(base).rglob('*') if p.is_file())
paths.update(Path(p) for p in ['Tools/check_sevra_mac.sh','Tools/check_sevra_memory_ui.sh','Tools/check_sevra_apps_ui.sh','Tools/check_sevra_thinking_ui.sh'])
allocated=sum(p.stat().st_blocks*512 for p in r.rglob('*') if p.is_file())
assert allocated+300_000_000<370_000_000_000
out.mkdir()
binpath=Path(subprocess.check_output(['swift','build','--package-path','apps/macos','-c','release','--show-bin-path'],text=True).strip())
shutil.copy2(binpath/'sevra-mac-checks',out/'sevra-mac-checks')
shutil.copy2('Tools/lib/mlx-0.32.2.metallib',out/'mlx.metallib')
with tarfile.open(out/'source.tar.gz','w:gz') as tar:
 for p in sorted(paths): tar.add(p,arcname=str(p),recursive=False)
identity={'schema':1,'scope':'Local development check binary, not a signed or published release. App source inputs match before and after the monitored full Mac suite. Check, native-check and CLI sources were captured afterward while unchanged. The wrapper engine binary hash is not the app executable; this receipt binds the actual check executable.', 'build_receipt_sha256':sha(run/'receipt.json'),'app_inputs':app,'source_files':{str(p):sha(p) for p in sorted(paths)},'source_archive_sha256':sha(out/'source.tar.gz'),'binary_sha256':sha(out/'sevra-mac-checks'),'metallib_sha256':sha(out/'mlx.metallib'),'research_allocated_bytes_before':allocated}
(out/'build-identity.json').write_text(json.dumps(identity,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in identity.items() if k not in ('app_inputs','source_files')}))
````
